Carga + HIS_raw (colapsable)\n","metadata":{}},{"id":"97d6b76c-2ff5-456b-b09d-18caa816edb8","cell_type":"code","source":"def his_raw(h_id, h_re):\n    \"\"\"Colapsa a 0 cuando <H>_re se aleja de <H>_id en escala |<H>_id|.\"\"\"\n    d = abs(h_id)\n    if d < EPS:\n        return float(\"nan\")\n    return max(0.0, 1.0 - abs(h_id - h_re) / d)\n\ndef make_workload(n, depth, idle_us):\n    qc = QuantumCircuit(n)\n    for layer in range(depth):\n        for i in range(n):\n            qc.h(i)\n            qc.rz(0.25 + 0.02 * layer, i)\n        for i in range(n - 1):\n            qc.cx(i, i + 1)\n        qc.barrier()\n        for i in range(n):\n            qc.delay(idle_us, i, unit=\"us\")\n        qc.barrier()\n    return qc\n\ndef make_H(n):\n    terms = []\n    for i in range(n - 1):\n        z = [\"I\"] * n\n        z[i], z[i + 1] = \"Z\", \"Z\"\n        terms.append((\"\".join(z), -0.5))\n        terms.append((\"I\" * n, 0.25 / max(n - 1, 1)))\n    return SparsePauliOp.from_list(terms).simplify()\n\nQC0 = make_workload(N, P, CONFIG[\"idle_us\"])\nH = make_H(N)\nH_IDEAL = float(np.real(Statevector.from_instruction(QC0).expectation_value(H)))\nprint(\"ops=%d depth=%d  <H>_ideal=%.4f\" % (QC0.size(), QC0.depth(), H_IDEAL))\nassert abs(H_IDEAL) > 0.1, \"ideal too small for HIS_raw\"\n\n# sanity: si <H>_re = 0 → HIS debe ser 0\nassert abs(his_raw(H_IDEAL, 0.0)) < 1e-9, \"HIS_raw no colapsa a 0 cuando H_re=0\"\nprint(\"SANITY OK: HIS_raw(H_id, 0) = %.4f  (debe ser 0)\" % his_raw(H_IDEAL, 0.0))\nprint(\"SANITY OK: HIS_raw(H_id, H_id) = %.4f (debe ser 1)\" % his_raw(H_IDEAL, H_IDEAL))\n\ndef estimate_H(counts, n):\n    labels = H.paulis.to_labels()\n    coeffs = np.real(H.coeffs)\n    total = sum(counts.values()) or 1\n    e = 0.0\n    for bs, c in counts.items():\n        s = bs.replace(\" \", \"\")[-n:].zfill(n)\n        val = 0.0\n        for lab, w in zip(labels, coeffs):\n            par = 0\n            for i, ch in enumerate(lab):\n                if ch != \"I\" and s[i] == \"1\":\n                    par ^= 1\n            val += w * (1 - 2 * par)\n        e += (c / total) * val\n    return e\n","metadata":{"trusted":true,"execution":{"iopub.status.busy":"2026-10-14T03:23:45.684618Z","iopub.execute_input":"2026-10-14T03:23:45.685830Z","iopub.status.idle":"2026-10-14T03:23:45.740099Z","shell.execute_reply.started":"2026-10-14T03:23:45.685776Z","shell.execute_reply":"2026-10-14T03:23:45.739265Z"}},"outputs":[{"name":"stdout","text":"ops=390 depth=120  <H>_ideal=-1.7342\nSANITY OK: HIS_raw(H_id, 0) = 0.0000  (debe ser 0)\nSANITY OK: HIS_raw(H_id, H_id) = 1.0000 (debe ser 1)\n","output_type":"stream"}],"execution_count":11},{"id":"b4383dac-b751-4106-8903-e4003e24133a","cell_type":"markdown","source":"## 3. pqr * 0.55, pqr * 0.55], [pqr * 0.45, 1 - pqr * 0.45]]), [q])\n        try:\n            tr = thermal_relaxation_error(t1q, t2q, 60.0)\n            nm.add_quantum_error(tr, [\"h\", \"rz\", \"id\", \"sx\", \"x\"], [q])\n        except Exception:\n            nm.add_quantum_error(depolarizing_error(pq1, 1), [\"h\", \"rz\", \"id\", \"sx\", \"x\"], [q])\n        try:\n            trd = thermal_relaxation_error(t1q, t2q, CONFIG[\"idle_us\"] * 1e3)\n            nm.add_quantum_error(trd, [\"delay\"], [q])\n        except Exception:\n            pass\n        nm.add_quantum_error(depolarizing_error(pq1, 1), [\"rz\"], [q])\n\n    for a in range(M -
